cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(stencil3d_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

add_executable(stencil3d stencil3d.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(stencil3d PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(stencil3d m)
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

// 7-point stencil computation
void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, 
                      std::vector<Real>& output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                
                const Real center = input[idx];
                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];
                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Copy boundary values
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                    output[idx] = input[idx];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);
    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        if (iter % 2 == 0) {
            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);
        } else {
            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);
        }
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

using Real = double;

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

// 7-point stencil computation
void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, 
                      std::vector<Real>& output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                
                const Real center = input[idx];
                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];
                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Copy boundary values
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                    output[idx] = input[idx];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);
    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        if (iter % 2 == 0) {
            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);
        } else {
            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);
        }
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

// 7-point stencil computation
void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, 
                      std::vector<Real>& output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                
                const Real center = input[idx];
                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];
                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Copy boundary values
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                    output[idx] = input[idx];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);
    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        if (iter % 2 == 0) {
            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);
        } else {
            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);
        }
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

import os, re, statistics, subprocess, sys
root=sys.argv[1]
for size,steps in [(128,200),(256,100),(512,20)]:
    args=['-x',str(size),'-i',str(steps)]
    original=[]
    for _ in range(3):
        out=subprocess.check_output([root+'/stencil3d-original',*args],text=True,timeout=30)
        original.append(int(re.search(r'Computation time: (\\d+) ms',out).group(1)))

    print(f'{size}"'^3 x {steps} iterations: original={reference} ms'"',flush=True)

        env=dict(os.environ,OMP_NUM_THREADS=str(threads),OMP_DYNAMIC='FALSE',OMP_PROC_BIND='spread',OMP_PLACES='cores')
        timings=[]
        for _ in range(3):
            out=subprocess.check_output([root+'/build/stencil3d',*args],text=True,env=env,timeout=30)
            timings.append(int(re.search(r'Computation time: (\\d+) ms',out).group(1)))
        elapsed=statistics.median(timings)
        print(f'  {threads:2} threads: {elapsed:5} ms, {reference/elapsed:.2f}x speedup (runs {timings})',flush=True)

+                        destination[idx] = (source[idx] + source[idx - 1] +
+                            source[idx + 1] + source[idx - nx] +
+                            source[idx + nx] + source[idx - plane] +
+                            source[idx + plane]) / 7.0;
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes this step before any thread
+            // reads the new input. Pointer swaps are private to each thread.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <memory>
#include <span>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

void initializeGrids(std::span<Real> grid1, std::span<Real> grid2,
                     const bool initializeSecond) {
    const size_t count = grid1.size();
    // Parallel first touch distributes both buffers across NUMA nodes. Both
    // buffers have identical fixed boundaries before the first iteration.
    #pragma omp parallel for simd schedule(static)
    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < count; ++idx) {
        const Real value = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
        grid1[idx] = value;
        grid2[idx] = initializeSecond ? value : 0.0;
    }
}

// 7-point stencil computation using one team for all time steps.
void stencilIterations(std::span<Real> grid1, std::span<Real> grid2,
                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                       const int iterations) {
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    const size_t interiorY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
    const size_t interiorZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
    const size_t lastX = nx > 1 ? nx - 1 : 1;

    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
        firstprivate(nx, plane, interiorY, interiorZ, lastX, iterations)
    {
        Real* input = grid1.data();
        Real* output = grid2.data();
        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
            const Real* __restrict source = input;
            Real* __restrict destination = output;

            // Collapsing rows exposes parallelism even for shallow grids,
            // while each thread traverses contiguous memory.
            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
            for (size_t z = 0; z < interiorZ; ++z) {
                for (size_t y = 0; y < interiorY; ++y) {
                    const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
                    #pragma omp simd
                    for (size_t x = 1; x < lastX; ++x) {
                        const size_t idx = row + x;
                        destination[idx] = (source[idx] + source[idx - 1] +
                            source[idx + 1] + source[idx - nx] +
                            source[idx + nx] + source[idx - plane] +
                            source[idx + plane]) / 7.0;
                    }
                }
            }
            // The worksharing barrier completes this step before any thread
            // reads the new input. Pointer swaps are private to each thread.
            std::swap(input, output);
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(std::span<const Real> grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // Check finiteness and bounds in a single parallel pass.
    int invalid = 0;
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    #pragma omp parallel for simd schedule(static) \
        reduction(|:invalid) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)
    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < grid.size(); ++idx) {
        const Real val = grid[idx];
        invalid |= !std::isfinite(val);
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    if (invalid) {
        printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Avoid serial value initialization so that the parallel initialization
    // determines page placement for both buffers.
    auto storage1 = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<Real[]>(gridSize);
    auto storage2 = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<Real[]>(gridSize);
    std::span<Real> grid1(storage1.get(), gridSize);
    std::span<Real> grid2(storage2.get(), gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrids(grid1, grid2, iterations > 0);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    stencilIterations(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz, iterations);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::span<const Real> finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename Range>
typename Range::value_type kahan_sum(const Range& data) {
    using T = typename Range::value_type;
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename Range>
void print_results(const Range& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    using T = typename Range::value_type;
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];

+void initializeGrids(std::span<Real> grid1, std::span<Real> grid2,

+// 7-point stencil computation using one team for all time steps.
+void stencilIterations(std::span<Real> grid1, std::span<Real> grid2,

+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t interiorY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;

+    const size_t lastX = nx > 1 ? nx - 1 : 1;

codex
The OpenMP build passes 264 exact comparisons with the original, covering varied grid shapes, odd and even iteration counts, and 1–8 threads. The compiler also confirms SIMD vectorization. For a 128³ grid over 200 iterations, the median compute time fell from 821 ms to 16 ms with 64 threads; I’m checking larger grids next.

The larger-grid runs scale well through 32 threads, but the 512³ grid slows at 64 threads, suggesting cache or memory bandwidth pressure. I’ll test spatial tiling to improve reuse of neighboring slices and expose more work when a grid has very few rows.

/bin/bash -lc 'cat stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp' in /home/llmtest/evals/20261006-204623/stencil3d_gpt-6.1-sol-xhigh_omp_r5

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <memory>
#include <span>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

void initializeGrids(std::span<Real> grid1, std::span<Real> grid2,
                     const bool initializeSecond) {
    const size_t count = grid1.size();
    // Parallel first touch distributes both buffers across NUMA nodes. Both
    // buffers have identical fixed boundaries before the first iteration.
    #pragma omp parallel for simd schedule(static)
    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < count; ++idx) {
        const Real value = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
        grid1[idx] = value;
        grid2[idx] = initializeSecond ? value : 0.0;
    }
}

// 7-point stencil computation using one team for all time steps.
void stencilIterations(std::span<Real> grid1, std::span<Real> grid2,
                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                       const int iterations) {
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    const size_t interiorY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
    const size_t interiorZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
    const size_t lastX = nx > 1 ? nx - 1 : 1;

    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
        firstprivate(nx, plane, interiorY, interiorZ, lastX, iterations)
    {
        Real* input = grid1.data();
        Real* output = grid2.data();
        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
            const Real* __restrict source = input;
            Real* __restrict destination = output;

            // Collapsing rows exposes parallelism even for shallow grids,
            // while each thread traverses contiguous memory.
            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
            for (size_t z = 0; z < interiorZ; ++z) {
                for (size_t y = 0; y < interiorY; ++y) {
                    const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
                    #pragma omp simd
                    for (size_t x = 1; x < lastX; ++x) {
                        const size_t idx = row + x;
                        destination[idx] = (source[idx] + source[idx - 1] +
                            source[idx + 1] + source[idx - nx] +
                            source[idx + nx] + source[idx - plane] +
                            source[idx + plane]) / 7.0;
                    }
                }
            }
            // The worksharing barrier completes this step before any thread
            // reads the new input. Pointer swaps are private to each thread.
            std::swap(input, output);
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(std::span<const Real> grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // Check finiteness and bounds in a single parallel pass.
    int invalid = 0;
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    #pragma omp parallel for simd schedule(static) \
        reduction(|:invalid) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)
    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < grid.size(); ++idx) {
        const Real val = grid[idx];
        invalid |= !std::isfinite(val);
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    if (invalid) {
        printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Avoid serial value initialization so that the parallel initialization
    // determines page placement for both buffers.
    auto storage1 = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<Real[]>(gridSize);
    auto storage2 = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<Real[]>(gridSize);
    std::span<Real> grid1(storage1.get(), gridSize);
    std::span<Real> grid2(storage2.get(), gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrids(grid1, grid2, iterations > 0);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    stencilIterations(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz, iterations);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::span<const Real> finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+// 7-point stencil computation using one team for all time steps.
+void stencilIterations(std::span<Real> grid1, std::span<Real> grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t interiorY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t interiorZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t lastX = nx > 1 ? nx - 1 : 1;
+    constexpr size_t tileX = 512;
+    constexpr size_t tileY = 16;
+    constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+    const size_t tilesX = (lastX - 1) / tileX + ((lastX - 1) % tileX != 0);
+    const size_t tilesY = interiorY / tileY + (interiorY % tileY != 0);
+    const size_t tilesZ = interiorZ / tileZ + (interiorZ % tileZ != 0);
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, interiorY, interiorZ, lastX, iterations, tilesX, tilesY, tilesZ)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            const Real* __restrict source = input;
+            Real* __restrict destination = output;
+
+            // Each tile's neighboring slices fit in private cache. Splitting
+            // all three dimensions also exposes work for long, shallow grids.
+            #pragma omp for collapse(3) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t bz = 0; bz < tilesZ; ++bz) {
+                for (size_t by = 0; by < tilesY; ++by) {
+                    for (size_t bx = 0; bx < tilesX; ++bx) {
+                        const size_t xBegin = 1 + bx * tileX;
+                        const size_t xEnd = std::min(xBegin + tileX, lastX);
+                        const size_t yBegin = 1 + by * tileY;
+                        const size_t yEnd = std::min(yBegin + tileY, interiorY + 1);
+                        const size_t zBegin = 1 + bz * tileZ;
+                        const size_t zEnd = std::min(zBegin + tileZ, interiorZ + 1);
+                        for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+                            for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+                                const size_t row = z * plane + y * nx;
+                                #pragma omp simd
+                                for (size_t x = xBegin; x < xEnd; ++x) {
+                                    const size_t idx = row + x;
+                                    destination[idx] = (source[idx] + source[idx - 1] +
+                                        source[idx + 1] + source[idx - nx] +
+                                        source[idx + nx] + source[idx - plane] +
+                                        source[idx + plane]) / 7.0;
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes this step before any thread
+            // reads the new input. Pointer swaps are private to each thread.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }

+void initializeGrids(std::span<Real> grid1, std::span<Real> grid2,

+// 7-point stencil computation using one team for all time steps.
+void stencilIterations(std::span<Real> grid1, std::span<Real> grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t interiorY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t interiorZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t lastX = nx > 1 ? nx - 1 : 1;
+    constexpr size_t tileX = 512;
+    constexpr size_t tileY = 16;
+    constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+    const size_t tilesX = (lastX - 1) / tileX + ((lastX - 1) % tileX != 0);
+    const size_t tilesY = interiorY / tileY + (interiorY % tileY != 0);
+    const size_t tilesZ = interiorZ / tileZ + (interiorZ % tileZ != 0);
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, interiorY, interiorZ, lastX, iterations, tilesX, tilesY, tilesZ)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            const Real* __restrict source = input;
+            Real* __restrict destination = output;
+
+            // Each tile's neighboring slices fit in private cache. Splitting
+            // all three dimensions also exposes work for long, shallow grids.
+            #pragma omp for collapse(3) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t bz = 0; bz < tilesZ; ++bz) {
+                for (size_t by = 0; by < tilesY; ++by) {
+                    for (size_t bx = 0; bx < tilesX; ++bx) {
+                        const size_t xBegin = 1 + bx * tileX;
+                        const size_t xEnd = std::min(xBegin + tileX, lastX);
+                        const size_t yBegin = 1 + by * tileY;
+                        const size_t yEnd = std::min(yBegin + tileY, interiorY + 1);
+                        const size_t zBegin = 1 + bz * tileZ;
+                        const size_t zEnd = std::min(zBegin + tileZ, interiorZ + 1);
+                        for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+                            for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+                                const size_t row = z * plane + y * nx;
+                                #pragma omp simd
+                                for (size_t x = xBegin; x < xEnd; ++x) {
+                                    const size_t idx = row + x;
+                                    destination[idx] = (source[idx] + source[idx - 1] +
+                                        source[idx + 1] + source[idx - nx] +
+                                        source[idx + nx] + source[idx - plane] +
+                                        source[idx + plane]) / 7.0;
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes this step before any thread
+            // reads the new input. Pointer swaps are private to each thread.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }

+void initializeGrids(std::span<Real> grid1, std::span<Real> grid2,
+                     const bool initializeSecond) {
+    const size_t count = grid1.size();
+    // Parallel first touch distributes both buffers across NUMA nodes. Both
+    // buffers have identical fixed boundaries before the first iteration.
+    #pragma omp parallel for simd schedule(static)
+    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < count; ++idx) {

-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
+// 7-point stencil computation using one team for all time steps.
+void stencilIterations(std::span<Real> grid1, std::span<Real> grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t interiorY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t interiorZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t lastX = nx > 1 ? nx - 1 : 1;
+    constexpr size_t tileX = 512;
+    constexpr size_t tileY = 16;
+    constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+    const size_t tilesX = (lastX - 1) / tileX + ((lastX - 1) % tileX != 0);
+    const size_t tilesY = interiorY / tileY + (interiorY % tileY != 0);
+    const size_t tilesZ = interiorZ / tileZ + (interiorZ % tileZ != 0);
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, interiorY, interiorZ, lastX, iterations, tilesX, tilesY, tilesZ)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            const Real* __restrict source = input;
+            Real* __restrict destination = output;
+
+            // Each tile's neighboring slices fit in private cache. Splitting
+            // all three dimensions also exposes work for long, shallow grids.
+            #pragma omp for collapse(3) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t bz = 0; bz < tilesZ; ++bz) {
+                for (size_t by = 0; by < tilesY; ++by) {
+                    for (size_t bx = 0; bx < tilesX; ++bx) {
+                        const size_t xBegin = 1 + bx * tileX;
+                        const size_t xEnd = std::min(xBegin + tileX, lastX);
+                        const size_t yBegin = 1 + by * tileY;
+                        const size_t yEnd = std::min(yBegin + tileY, interiorY + 1);
+                        const size_t zBegin = 1 + bz * tileZ;
+                        const size_t zEnd = std::min(zBegin + tileZ, interiorZ + 1);
+                        for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+                            for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+                                const size_t row = z * plane + y * nx;
+                                #pragma omp simd
+                                for (size_t x = xBegin; x < xEnd; ++x) {
+                                    const size_t idx = row + x;
+                                    destination[idx] = (source[idx] + source[idx - 1] +
+                                        source[idx + 1] + source[idx - nx] +
+                                        source[idx + nx] + source[idx - plane] +
+                                        source[idx + plane]) / 7.0;
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes this step before any thread
+            // reads the new input. Pointer swaps are private to each thread.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }

+// 7-point stencil computation using one team for all time steps.
+void stencilIterations(std::span<Real> grid1, std::span<Real> grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t interiorY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t interiorZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t lastX = nx > 1 ? nx - 1 : 1;
+    constexpr size_t tileX = 512;
+    constexpr size_t tileY = 16;
+    constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+    const size_t tilesX = (lastX - 1) / tileX + ((lastX - 1) % tileX != 0);
+    const size_t tilesY = interiorY / tileY + (interiorY % tileY != 0);
+    const size_t tilesZ = interiorZ / tileZ + (interiorZ % tileZ != 0);
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, interiorY, interiorZ, lastX, iterations, tilesX, tilesY, tilesZ)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            const Real* __restrict source = input;
+            Real* __restrict destination = output;
+
+            // Each tile's neighboring slices fit in private cache. Splitting
+            // all three dimensions also exposes work for long, shallow grids.
+            #pragma omp for collapse(3) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t bz = 0; bz < tilesZ; ++bz) {
+                for (size_t by = 0; by < tilesY; ++by) {
+                    for (size_t bx = 0; bx < tilesX; ++bx) {
+                        const size_t xBegin = 1 + bx * tileX;
+                        const size_t xEnd = std::min(xBegin + tileX, lastX);
+                        const size_t yBegin = 1 + by * tileY;
+                        const size_t yEnd = std::min(yBegin + tileY, interiorY + 1);
+                        const size_t zBegin = 1 + bz * tileZ;
+                        const size_t zEnd = std::min(zBegin + tileZ, interiorZ + 1);
+                        for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+                            for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+                                const size_t row = z * plane + y * nx;
+                                #pragma omp simd
+                                for (size_t x = xBegin; x < xEnd; ++x) {
+                                    const size_t idx = row + x;
+                                    destination[idx] = (source[idx] + source[idx - 1] +
+                                        source[idx + 1] + source[idx - nx] +
+                                        source[idx + nx] + source[idx - plane] +
+                                        source[idx + plane]) / 7.0;
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes this step before any thread
+            // reads the new input. Pointer swaps are private to each thread.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }

+// 7-point stencil computation using one team for all time steps.
+void stencilIterations(std::span<Real> grid1, std::span<Real> grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t interiorY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t interiorZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t lastX = nx > 1 ? nx - 1 : 1;
+    constexpr size_t tileX = 512;
+    constexpr size_t tileY = 16;
+    constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+    const size_t tilesX = (lastX - 1) / tileX + ((lastX - 1) % tileX != 0);
+    const size_t tilesY = interiorY / tileY + (interiorY % tileY != 0);
+    const size_t tilesZ = interiorZ / tileZ + (interiorZ % tileZ != 0);
+    // Small planes already reuse well in cache. Tile planes of at least 1 MiB
+    // or long rows when there are too few rows to occupy the requested team.
+    const bool useTiles = plane >= 1024 * 1024 / sizeof(Real) ||
+        (interiorY * interiorZ < static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()) && tilesX > 1);
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, interiorY, interiorZ, lastX, iterations, tilesX, tilesY, tilesZ, useTiles)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            const Real* source = input;
+            Real* destination = output;
+
+            if (useTiles) {
+                // Each tile's neighboring slices fit in private cache.
+                #pragma omp for collapse(3) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t bz = 0; bz < tilesZ; ++bz) {
+                    for (size_t by = 0; by < tilesY; ++by) {
+                        for (size_t bx = 0; bx < tilesX; ++bx) {
+                            const size_t xBegin = 1 + bx * tileX;
+                            const size_t xEnd = std::min(xBegin + tileX, lastX);
+                            const size_t yBegin = 1 + by * tileY;
+                            const size_t yEnd = std::min(yBegin + tileY, interiorY + 1);
+                            const size_t zBegin = 1 + bz * tileZ;
+                            const size_t zEnd = std::min(zBegin + tileZ, interiorZ + 1);
+                            for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+                                for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+                                    stencilRow(source, destination, z * plane + y * nx,
+                                               nx, plane, xBegin, xEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            } else {
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z = 0; z < interiorZ; ++z) {
+                    for (size_t y = 0; y < interiorY; ++y) {
+                        stencilRow(source, destination, (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx,
+                                   nx, plane, 1, lastX);
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes this step before any thread
+            // reads the new input. Pointer swaps are private to each thread.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }

+inline void stencilRow(const Real* __restrict source, Real* __restrict destination,
+                       const size_t row, const size_t nx, const size_t plane,
+                       const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = xBegin; x < xEnd; ++x) {
+        const size_t idx = row + x;
+        destination[idx] = (source[idx] + source[idx - 1] +
+            source[idx + 1] + source[idx - nx] +
+            source[idx + nx] + source[idx - plane] +
+            source[idx + plane]) / 7.0;
     }

-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
+// 7-point stencil computation using one team for all time steps.
+void stencilIterations(std::span<Real> grid1, std::span<Real> grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t interiorY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t interiorZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t lastX = nx > 1 ? nx - 1 : 1;
+    constexpr size_t tileX = 512;
+    constexpr size_t tileY = 16;
+    constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+    const size_t tilesX = (lastX - 1) / tileX + ((lastX - 1) % tileX != 0);
+    const size_t tilesY = interiorY / tileY + (interiorY % tileY != 0);
+    const size_t tilesZ = interiorZ / tileZ + (interiorZ % tileZ != 0);
+    // Small planes already reuse well in cache. Tile planes of at least 1 MiB
+    // or long rows when there are too few rows to occupy the requested team.
+    const bool useTiles = plane >= 1024 * 1024 / sizeof(Real) ||
+        (interiorY * interiorZ < static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()) && tilesX > 1);
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, interiorY, interiorZ, lastX, iterations, tilesX, tilesY, tilesZ, useTiles)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            const Real* source = input;
+            Real* destination = output;
+
+            if (useTiles) {
+                // Each tile's neighboring slices fit in private cache.
+                #pragma omp for collapse(3) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t bz = 0; bz < tilesZ; ++bz) {
+                    for (size_t by = 0; by < tilesY; ++by) {
+                        for (size_t bx = 0; bx < tilesX; ++bx) {
+                            const size_t xBegin = 1 + bx * tileX;
+                            const size_t xEnd = std::min(xBegin + tileX, lastX);
+                            const size_t yBegin = 1 + by * tileY;
+                            const size_t yEnd = std::min(yBegin + tileY, interiorY + 1);
+                            const size_t zBegin = 1 + bz * tileZ;
+                            const size_t zEnd = std::min(zBegin + tileZ, interiorZ + 1);
+                            for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+                                for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+                                    stencilRow(source, destination, z * plane + y * nx,
+                                               nx, plane, xBegin, xEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            } else {
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z = 0; z < interiorZ; ++z) {
+                    for (size_t y = 0; y < interiorY; ++y) {
+                        stencilRow(source, destination, (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx,
+                                   nx, plane, 1, lastX);
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes this step before any thread
+            // reads the new input. Pointer swaps are private to each thread.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }

codex
Tiling reduced the 512³ compute time at 64 threads from 310 ms to 234 ms. It added overhead on smaller grids, so I’ve kept row scheduling for those and enabled tiling for large planes or long rows with too little parallel work. Both paths use the same SIMD stencil and one barrier per iteration.
exec
/bin/bash -lc "python3 - '/tmp/stencil3d-check-PCASXV' <<'PY'
import os, re, subprocess, sys
root=sys.argv[1]
original=root+'/stencil3d-original'
parallel=root+'/build/stencil3d'
cases=[(1,1,1),(1,7,5),(2,8,9),(9,2,7),(8,7,2),(3,3,3),(5,7,9),(17,13,11),(65,9,5),(11,129,3),(128,128,128),(513,257,19),(1025,129,17),(4097,3,3),(65539,3,3)]
checks=0
for dims in cases:
    for steps in (-1,0,1,2,5,10):
        args=['-x',str(dims[0]),'-y',str(dims[1]),'-z',str(dims[2]),'-i',str(steps),'-r','-v']
        expected=subprocess.check_output([original,*args],text=True)
        expected=re.search(r'=== RESULTS ===.*=== END RESULTS ===',expected,re.S).group()
        for threads in (1,2,4,8):
            env=dict(os.environ,OMP_NUM_THREADS=str(threads),OMP_DYNAMIC='FALSE',OMP_PROC_BIND='spread',OMP_PLACES='cores')
            output=subprocess.check_output([parallel,*args],text=True,env=env,timeout=20)
            actual=re.search(r'=== RESULTS ===.*=== END RESULTS ===',output,re.S).group()
            if actual "'!= expected or '"'Validation: PASSED' not in output:
                raise SystemExit(f'Mismatch: dimensions={dims}, iterations={steps}, threads={threads}\\nExpected:\\n{expected}\\nActual:\\n{output}')
            checks+=1
print(f'PASS: {checks} exact result comparisons across 15 shapes, 6 iteration counts, and 4 thread counts, covering row and tile paths and partial tiles.',flush=True)
env={key:value for key,value in os.environ.items() if not key.startswith('OMP_')}
try:
    output=subprocess.check_output([parallel,'-v'],text=True,env=env,timeout=20)
    print('Default OpenMP configuration:\\n'+output,flush=True)
except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
    print('Default OpenMP configuration exceeded 20 seconds.',flush=True)

+inline void stencilRow(const Real* __restrict source, Real* __restrict destination,
+                       const size_t row, const size_t nx, const size_t plane,
+                       const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = xBegin; x < xEnd; ++x) {
+        const size_t idx = row + x;
+        destination[idx] = (source[idx] + source[idx - 1] +

+            source[idx + nx] + source[idx - plane] +
+            source[idx + plane]) / 7.0;

+// 7-point stencil computation using one team for all time steps.
+void stencilIterations(std::span<Real> grid1, std::span<Real> grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t interiorY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t interiorZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t lastX = nx > 1 ? nx - 1 : 1;
+    constexpr size_t tileX = 512;
+    constexpr size_t tileY = 16;
+    constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+    const size_t tilesX = (lastX - 1) / tileX + ((lastX - 1) % tileX != 0);
+    const size_t tilesY = interiorY / tileY + (interiorY % tileY != 0);
+    const size_t tilesZ = interiorZ / tileZ + (interiorZ % tileZ != 0);
+    // Small planes already reuse well in cache. Tile planes of at least 1 MiB
+    // or long rows when there are too few rows to occupy the requested team.
+    const bool useTiles = plane >= 1024 * 1024 / sizeof(Real) ||
+        (interiorY * interiorZ < static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()) && tilesX > 1);
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, interiorY, interiorZ, lastX, iterations, tilesX, tilesY, tilesZ, useTiles)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            const Real* source = input;
+            Real* destination = output;
+
+            if (useTiles) {
+                // Each tile's neighboring slices fit in private cache.
+                #pragma omp for collapse(3) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t bz = 0; bz < tilesZ; ++bz) {
+                    for (size_t by = 0; by < tilesY; ++by) {
+                        for (size_t bx = 0; bx < tilesX; ++bx) {
+                            const size_t xBegin = 1 + bx * tileX;
+                            const size_t xEnd = std::min(xBegin + tileX, lastX);
+                            const size_t yBegin = 1 + by * tileY;
+                            const size_t yEnd = std::min(yBegin + tileY, interiorY + 1);
+                            const size_t zBegin = 1 + bz * tileZ;
+                            const size_t zEnd = std::min(zBegin + tileZ, interiorZ + 1);
+                            for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+                                for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+                                    stencilRow(source, destination, z * plane + y * nx,
+                                               nx, plane, xBegin, xEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            } else {
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z = 0; z < interiorZ; ++z) {
+                    for (size_t y = 0; y < interiorY; ++y) {
+                        stencilRow(source, destination, (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx,
+                                   nx, plane, 1, lastX);
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes this step before any thread
+            // reads the new input. Pointer swaps are private to each thread.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }

+// 7-point stencil computation using one team for all time steps.
+void stencilIterations(std::span<Real> grid1, std::span<Real> grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t interiorY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t interiorZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t lastX = nx > 1 ? nx - 1 : 1;
+    constexpr size_t tileX = 512;
+    constexpr size_t tileY = 16;
+    constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+    const size_t tilesX = (lastX - 1) / tileX + ((lastX - 1) % tileX != 0);
+    const size_t tilesY = interiorY / tileY + (interiorY % tileY != 0);
+    const size_t tilesZ = interiorZ / tileZ + (interiorZ % tileZ != 0);
+    // Small planes already reuse well in cache. Tile planes of at least 1 MiB
+    // or long rows when there are too few rows to occupy the requested team.
+    const bool useTiles = plane >= 1024 * 1024 / sizeof(Real) ||
+        (interiorY * interiorZ < static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()) && tilesX > 1);
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, interiorY, interiorZ, lastX, iterations, tilesX, tilesY, tilesZ, useTiles)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            const Real* source = input;
+            Real* destination = output;
+
+            if (useTiles) {
+                // Each tile's neighboring slices fit in private cache.
+                #pragma omp for collapse(3) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t bz = 0; bz < tilesZ; ++bz) {
+                    for (size_t by = 0; by < tilesY; ++by) {
+                        for (size_t bx = 0; bx < tilesX; ++bx) {
+                            const size_t xBegin = 1 + bx * tileX;
+                            const size_t xEnd = std::min(xBegin + tileX, lastX);
+                            const size_t yBegin = 1 + by * tileY;
+                            const size_t yEnd = std::min(yBegin + tileY, interiorY + 1);
+                            const size_t zBegin = 1 + bz * tileZ;
+                            const size_t zEnd = std::min(zBegin + tileZ, interiorZ + 1);
+                            for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+                                for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+                                    stencilRow(source, destination, z * plane + y * nx,
+                                               nx, plane, xBegin, xEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            } else {
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z = 0; z < interiorZ; ++z) {
+                    for (size_t y = 0; y < interiorY; ++y) {
+                        stencilRow(source, destination, (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx,
+                                   nx, plane, 1, lastX);
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes this step before any thread
+            // reads the new input. Pointer swaps are private to each thread.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }

+inline void stencilRow(const Real* __restrict source, Real* __restrict destination,
+                       const size_t row, const size_t nx, const size_t plane,
+                       const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd) {
+    #pragma omp simd

-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
+// 7-point stencil computation using one team for all time steps.
+void stencilIterations(std::span<Real> grid1, std::span<Real> grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t interiorY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t interiorZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t lastX = nx > 1 ? nx - 1 : 1;
+    constexpr size_t tileX = 512;
+    constexpr size_t tileY = 16;
+    constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+    const size_t tilesX = (lastX - 1) / tileX + ((lastX - 1) % tileX != 0);
+    const size_t tilesY = interiorY / tileY + (interiorY % tileY != 0);
+    const size_t tilesZ = interiorZ / tileZ + (interiorZ % tileZ != 0);
+    // Small planes already reuse well in cache. Tile planes of at least 1 MiB
+    // or long rows when there are too few rows to occupy the requested team.
+    const bool useTiles = plane >= 1024 * 1024 / sizeof(Real) ||
+        (interiorY * interiorZ < static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()) && tilesX > 1);
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, interiorY, interiorZ, lastX, iterations, tilesX, tilesY, tilesZ, useTiles)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            const Real* source = input;
+            Real* destination = output;
+
+            if (useTiles) {
+                // Each tile's neighboring slices fit in private cache.
+                #pragma omp for collapse(3) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t bz = 0; bz < tilesZ; ++bz) {
+                    for (size_t by = 0; by < tilesY; ++by) {
+                        for (size_t bx = 0; bx < tilesX; ++bx) {
+                            const size_t xBegin = 1 + bx * tileX;
+                            const size_t xEnd = std::min(xBegin + tileX, lastX);
+                            const size_t yBegin = 1 + by * tileY;
+                            const size_t yEnd = std::min(yBegin + tileY, interiorY + 1);
+                            const size_t zBegin = 1 + bz * tileZ;
+                            const size_t zEnd = std::min(zBegin + tileZ, interiorZ + 1);
+                            for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+                                for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+                                    stencilRow(source, destination, z * plane + y * nx,
+                                               nx, plane, xBegin, xEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            } else {
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z = 0; z < interiorZ; ++z) {
+                    for (size_t y = 0; y < interiorY; ++y) {
+                        stencilRow(source, destination, (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx,
+                                   nx, plane, 1, lastX);
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // The worksharing barrier completes this step before any thread
+            // reads the new input. Pointer swaps are private to each thread.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }
